PCW — Card

Fusion 39, 15 June 2001

15 June 2001
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Fusion 39 15 Jun 2001 Fargo Dome, Fargo, North Dakota A drinks machine and Miyagawa's opening assault The lighting is dim. Mainly because the jackasses at The Fargo Dome in Fargo, North Dakota don’t know how to change a light bulb. Ricky Flame stands there next to a drinks machine. He puts a couple quarters into it and hits a button. Nothing. He hits the button again. Still nothing. Again… nothing. He thumps the machine on the side.Ricky: Give me my f’cking drink you b’stard machine! Suddenly Miyagawa rushes onto the screen, catching Flame by the back of the head and slamming him straight into the front of the machine, shattering the front, and dropping Flame straight to the floor. Miyagawa looks a little more… well… peeved than usual. He suddenly stops the attack as Flame lays in a pool of blood.Miyagawa: You know what? You want to know what really… really… really…. REALLY PISSES ME OFF, FLAME?!!!Ricky just stays on the floor groaning.Miyagawa: I’ll tell you… My theme tune is One Step Closer. My theme has ALWAYS BEEN One Step Closer. Two men before have tried to use MY theme, Flame. One was Pheonix, and I ended up driving him to PCW2. The other was a man by the name of Archangel… and he left even quicker after I started my assault.Miya steps around Flame’s body.Miyagawa: Well I like to enjoy a good meal, you see. With a good meal you take your time eating it, you spread it out over a couple of hours rather than wolfing it down in minutes. Well Flame, let me tell you this… welcome to the best meal of my life, AND THE VERY WORST DAY OF YOURS~!As Miyagawa strides off the Friday Fusion intro starts up… The usual opening for Friday Fusion depicting PCW Wrestlers in action amidst a field of green lightning and purple explosions begins to roll. Wrestlers highlighted are Jeremy “Krayzie” Howard, PCW Cruiserweight Champion Seth Jackon, Meanstreak and The Evil Bastard, otherwise known as The Sadistic Bastard, otherwise known simply as “Clyde”.Dave: Ok, I’m not gonna say “Hey Folks, welcome to Fusion”… I’m going to go straight into it…. What the hell was that crap? Who’s dumbass idea was it to start off Fusion with a Miyagawa promo? Couldn’t we get at least I dunno… at least…Steven: Boca?Jeff: I don’t think we’re that despirate. Of course we could invite Jak Nemesis back.Dave: Look, action in the crowd!The camera cuts into the crowd where a group of former PCW wrestlers are waving their application forms for the new PCW in the air as they are being battered by PCW security guards. Such wrestlers as the previously mentioned Jak Nemesis, Big Gooey Sac and the famous Hans Kruger. Suddenly Kruger kicks a guard in the nuts, and the rest of the guards avoid him like the plague and let him watch Fusion in peace…. All the while attack Sac and Nemesis more brutally than the Italian Police at a World Cup Qualifier.Jeff: ENGLISH CARD WRITER ALERT! ENGLISH CARD WRITER ALERT!Dave: Right guv’ we getting on with the next match then geezer?Jeff: We rightly are…Dave: Ok, it's time for the first match of the evening here on Fusion.Jeff: Wake me up when it's done. I can't sit through this."Du Hast" by Rammstein hits as Aleister Crowley makes his way to the ring.Clay: First, coming down the ring aisle, weighing at a 369 lbs. from Yo Momma's Bed.......... ALEISTER CROWLEY!!!!!!!!!Jeff: Just great, another Mafia Inc. guy who's going to want a shot at the World title on Sabotage.Dave: The World title is already up for grabs on Sabotage.Jeff: Looks like Crowley's going to be gone in no time, he's going to whine his butt out of here.Dave: Oh, would you shut up already. Give the man a chance already.Steve: I'll give him a chance alright."Self High Five" hits as Drummer makes his way to the ring.Clay: And his opponent, weighing in at 220 lbs. from Seattle, Washington............. DRUMMER!!!!!!!!!!Jeff: DOUG TROY JOBBED TO HANK! HANK I SAY! HANK~!!Dave: Ok, ok, we get the picture. How many times you going to say that during every show we do?Jeff: Sorry, it was a once in a lifetime feat for Hank.Dave: I guess that’s fair enough.Jeff: Now all we need is Bad Battio to come back, and I can start shouting other comments occasionally.Dave: You don’t mind being stereotyped, do you?Jeff: Not in the least.Drummer hits the ring, and the bigger Crowley begins laying the boots to him. Drummer is on the defensive early, trying to get away from the massive boots of Crowley. Crowley picks up Drummer in a double chokeslam, while Drummer kicks his feet, trying to break loose. Crowley just heaves Drummer through the air into the corner.Dave: It appears that Drummer is going to have his work cut out for him in this match.Jeff: Unfortuantly you can’t win every match by hitting your opponent with a bottle of SOBE~! Crowley picks up Drummer and sends him into the ropes. He goes for a big boot to the face of Drummer, but Drummer ducks under, hits the other ropes and lands a spinning heel kick to the face of Crowley. Crowley starts to stagger backwards. Drummer goes up to the second rope and comes off with an elbow to the forehead of Crowley. Crowley goes down to one knee. Drummer hits the ropes again, and then delivers a short dropkick to the back of Crowley's head. Dave: Drummer is on fire in this first match. Steve: My pants are on fire from last night. Jeff: SHUT UP! BIGGETH SMACKETH~! Dave: Thanks Jeff. Drummer lifts Crowley's head up into a DDT position. He jumps to the second rope and goes for a tilt a whirl DDT, but Crowley uses his strength and tosses Drummer off of him. Drummer lands on his face, on the other side of the ropes. Dave: Crowley is just too big for Hank. Drummer's in big trouble in this match. Jeff: He beat Doug Troy, he can beat Aleister Crowley. Dave: He'd better come up with a better offense than that then. Crowley walks over to Drummer and picks him up with a airplane spin. Crowley spins Drummer around and around and around, before he sends him crashing to the canvas. Drummer grabs his stomach in pain. Dave: That had to knock the wind out of Drummer there. Landing on the mat from eight feet in the air. Jeff: Serves him right for picking on someone bigger than he is. Dave: You're always so quick to jump ship aren't you? Steve: I'm always quick to jump... SMACK! Crowley lifts Drummer back up and sends him into the ropes. Drummer bounces off and sidesteps Crowley and locks on the sleeper hold. Crowley reaches out with everything he has to reach for air. Drummer cinches it on. Crowley can't escape it. The ref checks the hand the first time. It drops. Then a second time, it almost drops, but Crowley's hand goes shooting into the air. The bigger Crowley just lifts up Drummer and executes a back body drop. Dave: I'm telling you, Crowley is too much for Drummer. Jeff: I told you so. Dave: Sure you did. Problem is, I just don't remember. Jeff: Doh! Crowley picks up Drummer again. He slaps him into an inverted DDT and drops him. Crowley makes the cover. The ref counts, 1.. 2.. KICK OUT! Drummer kicked out just in the nick of time. Crowley is beside himself. He picks up Drummer and throws him into the ropes. Drummer ducks the clothesline by Crowley and slides to the outside of the ring. Crowley goes to follow, but Drummer grabs his feet and pulls the big man out. Crowley hits the mat hard. Drummer climbs back in the ring, and goes to the top rope. Crowley lays half draped on the outside of the ring, as Drummer comes off with a guillotine legdrop. Dave: Drummer is taking it to Crowley now. Jeff: It's all that jobber luck he seems to be running on. Drummer lifts Crowley to his feet and hits a side Russian legsweep. Crowley hits the mat and bounces right back up. Drummer stands in shock. Drummer bounces off the ropes and goes for a clothesline on the 300 pounder, but Crowley stands in one place and smiles. Jeff: Duh Drummer, that's not going to do it. Dave: The bigger Crowley just laughs at Drummer. He'll have to do better than that. Jeff: Isn't that what I just said. Steve. Not really. What you just said was....... Jeff: SHUT UP! Crowley reaches out and grabs Drummer. Drummer tries to wiggle away. But Crowley throws him into the ropes and hits him with a big boot to the face. Dave: Crowley hit what he calls the Ground Zero. Jeff: Drummer keeps this up, and that is exactly what he's going to hit. Steve: I'd like to hit that with him. SMACK. Crowley picks up Drummer and executes a running powerbomb on him. He begins to go for a second running powerbomb, but out of the back runs Michael Cade. Cade hits the ring and hits his Five Star Kick on Crowley. Dave: MICHEAL CADE HAS JUST HIT THE FIVE STAR KICK ON CROWLEY. CADE IS HUNTING FOR TROUBLE NOW! Cade reaches over to Drummer and puts his right arm over a knocked out Crowley. The ref makes the count. 1... 2... 3!!!!!!!!!!!!!! Winner: HANK~! Erm… DRUMMER~! Jeff: Crowley just jobbed to Hank! HANK!!!!!! Dave: CADE HIT THE FIVE STAR KICK ON CROWLEY AND COST HIM THE MATCH. Michael Cade walks back up the ramp, smiling at his job well done. Dave: Michael Cade just cost Crowley his match with Drummer, and now he must immediately fight Jon Kano. No easy task there. Jeff: I know, I know, KANO ROCKS! Crowd: KANO ROCKS, KANO ROCKS! Dave: I guess the crowd has spoken. "See me Rollin'" by Kid Rock hits as Micheal Cade makes his way to the ring. Clay: First, coming down the ring aisle, weighing in at 230 from Miami, Florida............. MICHAEL CADE Dave: I don't know how smart this is, staying out here after costing the big man, Aleister Crowley a match against Drummer. Jeff: Aleister Crowley jobbed to HANK~! Dave: I wouldn't yell that too loud if I was you. Steven: My friend was yelling alot last night. It was because I was... Dave: SHUT UP! "Come and Get Me" by Jay-Z hits as Jon Kano makes his way to the ring. Crowd: KANO ROCKS! KANO ROCKS! Clay: And his opponent, making his way to the ring at 297 lbs. from Las Vegas, Nevada............ JON KANO Kano hits the ring and both men begin throwing punches at each other. Kano takes a left, then he delivers a right to Cade. Cade steps back, but not in time to avoid the clothesline from Kano. Kano sends him int a the ropes, Cade bounces off into Kano's high knee. Kano quickly reaches down for Cade. He locks him in a dragon sleeper. Dave: Kano is really taking it to Cade. Jeff: Ah, he's just biding his time. Then Cade will show Kano just how much he really doesn't rock. Dave: Well if he doesn't rock so much, then why do you keep on saying it? Jeff: To be cool, why else? Steve: I say it because I...... SMACK! Steve: I didn't even get to finish that ti......... SMACK! Cade appears to fading out, but just when Kano thought he had him. Cade flips him over. Then Cade runs the ropes and drops a leg onto Kano's throat. Jeff: I told you Cade had it in him. Dave: Kano's just biding his time, then he's going for the kill. Crowd: KANO ROCKS! KANO ROCKS! Cade picks up Kano, and hits a belly to belly double arm suplex. Kano hits the mat hard, grabbing his back. Cade jaw jacks with the crowd a bit for locking on a cobra clutch on a fallen Kano. Kano reaches out for air, but to no avail. Cade cinches in on it. The ref check's Kano's hand. It drops. The ref checks for a second time, it drops. He goes for a third, but Kano shoots to a knee and delivers an elbow to the breadbasket of Cade. Cade is reeling. Dave: Kano has come back to life! Kano breaks free and bounces off the ropes, then hits Cade with a forearm to the side of Cade's head. Cade falls through the ropes. Kano follows to the outside. Kano grabs Cade's head and slams it into the ring steps, then he tosses him back into the ring. Dave: Scrambled eggs please. Jeff: Cade will stilll come back. I know he will. Steve: I'll come, I'll come. SMACK! Kano grabs Cade in a chokeslam, Cade tries to wiggle free. He can't. Kano hits him with the running chokeslam. Cade is nearly out. Dave: Kano is signaling for the end. Jeff: He won't get it. Steve: I'll get it...... Dave: SHUT UP! Kano picks up Cade for the overhead rockbottom, or Kanoplex, he almost hits it, but Cade reverses it into a tornado DDT. Both men lay on the mat, out cold. The ref starts the mandatory ten count. 1.... 2..... 3.... 4...... 5..... Cade makes it to his knee, Kano is just stirring. 6..... 7..... Cade is almost up. 8..... 9..... Cade hits Kano with a clothesling to send him back to the mat. Jeff: I told you so! Dave: SHUT UP! Cade has Kano in the Argentine back breaker rack, but Kano won't submit. The ref keeps asking Kano, but he still won't give. Cade drops him. Cade stands off in the corner and signals for the five star kick. Kano starts to rise to both feet. Cade gets ready and.......... Dave: OH MY GOD! CHOKESLAM FROM ALEISTER CROWLEY. PAYBACK FOR EARLIER! Jeff: That's not fair. The ref never saw it, because Kano had him distracted for some reason. This is not cool. Kano walks over to Cade and hits the Kanoplex on him. He covers and the ref makes the count. 1..... 2..... 3!!!!!! Winner: Jon Kano! Dave: KANO WINS BECAUSE OF CROWLEY'S CHOKESLAM. A LITTLE TICK FOR TACK FOR EARLIER!!!!!!!!!! Jeff: Is it time for the next match, I'm disgusted with Fusion so far. Dave: Not quite yet, I’m hearing that Flame is back up. Jeff: Damn. Dave: And he’s being interviewed with Cal Seaver out the back. Jeff: Ok, let’s go see what Flamey and Spankey have to say. The camera cuts out the back to the interview stage where Cal Seaver stands ready to talk to Ricky Flame. Flame seems to have a small bandage on his forehead where he went head first into the drinks machine. Cal: We all saw your awful attack earlier, what do you have to say on the fact that you didn’t even manage to get one punch in? Ricky: HEY! Cal: And the fact that you looked really quite like a wuss? Ricky: HEY! You shut your mouth Cal! I beat that jackass silly on Tuesday, and if he tries it on again tonight, I’ll just beat him stupid again, just like I did when I became number one contender to the Extreme title. Miyagawa: Hey jackass! Ricky turn’s his head and see’s Miyagawa on a small TV monitor. Miyagawa: If you are watching this message, then I am currently standing behind you with a shovel… by the way… BOO! The real Miyagawa suddenly charges on scene and twats Ricky in the back of the head with a shovel. Ricky goes down like dead weight. Miyagawa smacks him in the back of the head again… and again… and again…. Before tossing the shovel to Cal, who catches it reflexes. Miyagawa seems quite calm as he begins to speak to Cal. Miyagawa: Heya Cal, how’s it hanging? Cal: Erm… yeah… s’alright. Miyagawa: Cool… I’ll be on my way, now I’ve had the starter, I shall go and find the main course, and then start choosing the best part of all… desert. Cal: Was that supposed to sound menacing? Miyagawa: It was pretty crappy wasn’t it? The camera cuts back to the announce desk. Jeff: Shoot me, shoot me now. Dave: Hey at least one good thing is coming out of this. Jeff: Which is? Dave: Well rumour has it that the real Phantom Booker and Miyagawa are in cohorts. Because Flame tried to negotiate perks in his PCW Contract, TPB is getting Miyagawa to kick the crap out of him. Jeff: Nah, that’s a load of balls. Let’s get to our next match. Clay Clayborne: The following contest is for the right to use the nickname “The Real Deal” in PCW. Introducing first, the special guest referee… “Greed” by Godsmack blasts over the PA as the CEO of PCW Tyler Nelson makes his way out from the back in a pair of black warm up pants and referee shirt. Clay Clayborne: …he is the CEO of Primetime Championship Wrestling and former multiple PCW World Champion…TYLER NELSON!! The CEO makes his way down and climbs into the ring. He retreats to a far corner and awaits the combatants. Clay Clayborne: Now, the first contestant making his way toward the ring….MATT WILSON!! Matt Wilson makes his way down, sans music. Some of the fans begin to chant “Steve Lombardi” at him as he climbs in the ring. He walks toward the CEO who turns his back to him, snickering at the man with no music. Dave: What’s up with these guys that have no entrance music? Jeff: It’s all part of being a jobber. I think it’s actually a requirement that you have no music. Dave: What about the jobbers that have the cheesy generic guitar riffs that they come to the ring to? Jeff: For once you make a valid point, Dave. I forgot about the musical jobbers like the Armstrong Brothers and Mr. Showtime. Clay Clayborne: And his opponent… “Change the Game” by Jay-Z pumps over the PA as Shawn Bell makes his way down the ramp to a rather impressive pop from the crowd. Clay Clayborne: …hailing from Halifax, Nova Scotia, Canada and weighing in at 211 pounds…SHAWN BELL!! Bell approaches the ring, his gaze fixed on the CEO. Dave: Shawn Bell certainly has a chip on his shoulder where the CEO is concerned. Jeff: If he doesn’t watch it Mr. Nelson will knock that chip off along with his head. Bell slides under the bottom rope and walks toward the CEO who points at his referee shirt and shakes his head. Bell talks some smack and then gives Nelson the finger. This draws a huge pop from the crowd. Dave: Shawn Bell letting the CEO know exactly what he thinks of him. Jeff: Who cares what this guy thinks. He’s fighting for the use of a nickname for God’s sake! How much more jobberific can you get? Nelson calls for the bell and the two “Real Deals” circle the ring. As Bell and Wilson lock up in a collar and elbow tie up, Nelson hopes out of the ring and makes his way to the announce table. Jeff: Move it Steven so the CEO can sit down! Steven: He can sit on my lap if he wants. A thunderous smack is heard followed by the sound of a headset being fumbled around. Steven stands off to the side as Tyler takes a seat next to Jeff. Jeff: Welcome to the announce position, Mr. Nelson! Dave: Jesus, Jeff! Can you kiss any more ass? Tyler: Dave, I expect the same type of treatment from you or you’ll be living in the projects with Da Westsidas living off of food stamps. Dave: (mumbling under his breath) Welcome Mr. Nelson. In the ring Bell gains the advantage and whips Wilson into the ropes. Bell catches Wilson with a Samoan Drop and immediately hops to his feet and jaw jacks the CEO. Tyler: Look at this chump. He actually thinks he has some skills. Jeff: I know! What is the world coming to? Tyler: I tell you what, Bell better keep an eye on this Wilson guy. He’s scrappy. Jeff: Really? Tyler: No. Bell turns his attention back to Wilson and starts to drop some right hands to his jaw. Bell backs Wilson into the corner and continues the advantage with a series of chops and kicks that have Wilson hanging on to the top rope to stay on his feet. Bell grabs Wilson’s arm and whips him across to the opposite corner. Wilson slams into the turnbuckles and staggers out a few steps from the impact. Bell shoots himself into the ropes behind Wilson, points at the CEO, and then drives Wilson into the mat face first with a bulldog. Dave: That was impressive by Bell. Tyler: No it wasn’t, Dave. Impressive is being the two time PCW World Champion. Impressive is being the longest reigning Television Champion in PCW history. Impressive is being a part of the most dominating group in wrestling history, Greed, INC. Jeff: YES! Lay the verbal punkage down, Supreme CEO! Dave: (sigh) Tyler: And what the hell is up with all of those sighs? I pay you to talk, not sigh like a teenager girl who just got grounded from the phone. Dave: …….. Bell doesn’t even bother with a cover since the referee is doing commentary at ringside. He pulls Wilson up and perches him up on the top turnbuckle. Bell climbs to the second rope and lands several right hands to the head of Wilson, then gets Wilson in a front face lock and drapes an arm over his own head. Dave: Wilson going for the ride! Bell lifts Wilson up, looks down at the CEO, then takes him over with a huge superplex. Bell now makes a cover but the CEO doesn’t move to get in the ring. Crowd: BOOOOOO!!!! Dave: Aren’t you going to go make the count? You are the referee you know! Tyler: Dave, you’re treading on thin ice tonight. I have long been unimpressed by your performance out here. I’m sure there are plenty of people that would love to have your job. Shall we find out? Dave: (angrily) That won’t be necessary. Bell gets to his feet and stands on the side of the ring overlooking the announce table. He points in an animated fashion and shouts at the CEO who simply looks up at him and grins. Tyler: Does this guy realize that I don’t give two sh*ts about him? Jeff: The guy is about as mentally sharp as a marble. Bell is getting more and more frustrated at the CEO’s seeming non-interest and slams his hands down on the top rope. Suddenly he leaps up on the top rope and springboards off towards the announce table. Jeff: LOOK OUT MR. NELSON!! Jeff lunges in front of the CEO and takes the brunt of the summersault body attack by Bell. Crowd: PCW!! PCW!! PCW!! PCW!! Dave: HOLY SH*T!! SHAWN BELL JUST TOOK OUT JEFF MARX AS HE WAS GOING AFTER THE CEO TYLER NELSON! Marx and Bell lay behind the announce table in a heap as the CEO removes his headset and starts to put the boots to Bell. Tyler drags Bell to his feet and whips him into the steel ring post. Bell hits with a loud thud and falls to the floor. Crowd: ASSHOLE!! ASSHOLE!! ASSHOLE!! Dave: THE CEO, WHO IS THE REFEREE, IS ATTACKING SHAWN BELL!! Tyler drags Bell up once again and rolls him into the ring. The CEO follows him in and pulls him to his feet by his hair, pointing a finger in his face and yelling some undecipherable obscenities. Then Tyler scoops him up on his shoulders and drops him with the HOSTILE TAKEOVER! Dave: THIS IS AN OUTRAGE! NELSON CAN”T DO THAT!! Tyler rolls Bell over on top of Wilson and then makes a count…1…2…3!! Winner: Shawn “The Real Deal” Bell Time: 4:57 The CEO stands over Bell, pointing at himself as if to say ‘You won because I let you win’. Suddenly there is a pop from the crowd. Dave: IT”S JON KANO!! JON KANO IS COMING OUT TO HELP SHAWN BELL!! Kano sprints down to the ring and slides under the bottom rope. Tyler pounces on him and starts to land clubbing right and left hands to the back of Kano. As the CEO continues to attack Kano fights to his feet and starts to go toe to toe with Tyler. Kano is able to block a shot from the CEO and lands several unanswered right hands to the jaw of Nelson. Bell is stirring on the mat and gets to his feet, joining Kano in the attack on Tyler. The two men whip Nelson into the ropes and nearly take his head off with a double clothesline. Then they start to put the boots to the CEO as the crowd roars its apporval. Dave: KICK HIS ASS!! The cheering is short lived however as the rest of Greed, INC rush to the ring and immediately subdue the two attackers. Badge and Yoder take Kano and trap him in a corner, proceeding to pummel him to the mat. Ryan Knox and Jack McGriff grab Shawn Bell and Knox nails him with a swinging neckbreaker. McGriff follows up with a knee drop across the forehead of Bell. Knox and Bell then start to stomp the proverbial mudhole in Shawn Bell as Nelson stands with a look of utter contempt on his face. He barks out for Knox and McGriff to pick Bell up off the mat. They comply and Tyler gets right in Bells face and spits in it, then grabs him by the head, spins around, and leans forward lifting Bell off the ground by his head with GREED IS GOOD!! Dave: SOMEONE GET OUT HERE AND STOP THIS MADNESS!! Jeff: (groggily) Get that bastard for me, Mr. Nelson! Tyler holds Bell up for a few seconds more then unceremoniously drops him to the mat. Badge and Yoder kick Kano out of the ring to the floor and the men in the ring do their best to dodge the incoming debris being thrown by the crowd. Then they realize that Matt Wilson is still down in the ring and all of them stand over him as the CEO calls for a microphone. Nelson: Look at this worthless piece of sh*t! Who even hired this guy in the first place? I’m going to have to have it out with Dorkiman and Porter if this is what they call quality talent. Tyler motions to Badge and Yoder. Nelson: Wake him up! The two huge men drag Wilson to his feet and smack him around a few times until his eyes are opened. Nelson: Hey! Listen very carefully. Seeing as how you can’t even beat Shawn Bell, I think that your services are no longer required here in PCW. You can now refer to yourself as “No Deal” Matt Wilson because you are CANCELLED!! Tyler drops the microphone and motions once again to Badge and Yoder. They grab Wilson by the throat and hoist him up in the air. They walk over to the side of the ring and give Matt Wilson a DOUBLE CHOKESLAM FROM THE RING TO THE ARENA FLOOR!! Crowd: BOOOO!!!! Dave: GREED, INC JUST KILLED MATT WILSON AFTER THE CEO FIRED HIM!! SHAWN BELL AND JON KANO WERE ALSO LAID TO WASTE BY THE CEO AND HIS HENCHMEN! WE NEED TO CUT TO COMMERCIAL! {Commercial: Steelramp.com They might not be 100% perfect, but they at least aren’t the WWW Nazis like Homestead} Dave: Let’s get right back to it. Jeff: No Miyagawa beating up Flame? Dave: He must be having a piss or something. Steven: Can… Jeff & Dave: NO! Clay Clayborne: The following match is a no disqualification, no time limit contest. “Crazy Train” by Ozzy Osbourne blasts over the PA as Kayce Jade makes his way down the ramp. Clay Clayborne: Introducing first…from Seattle, Washington and weighing 240 pounds…KAYCE JADE!! “Welcome to the Jungle” by Guns n Roses hits over the PA as Tommy Christopher comes out from the back. Clay Clayborne: And his opponent…from Santa Monica, California…he weighs 223 pounds…”SENSATIONAL” TOMMY CHRISTOPHER!! Dave: These two had one heck of a battle last time and now look to settle the war. Jeff: So what you really mean is that we are going to suffer through these two jobbers again? Dave: (sighs) However you want to interpret it, Jeff. TC rolls in the ring and he and KJ stare each other down. The bell sounds to start the match and TC rolls out of the ring to the floor. He looks under the ring and digs around for a few seconds. Crowd: WOOD!! WOOD!! WOOD!! TC obliges and pulls a table out from under the ring. He sets it up at ringside. Dave: This match has barely started and already there are tables being set up! KJ also slides out of the ring on the opposite side and pulls a table out from under the ring. He also sets his up as TC climbs back in the ring. KJ rolls in and TC attacks him with a flurry of boots to the head and back. TC pulls KJ up and kicks him in the stomach before muscling him up for a butterfly backbreaker. KJ arches his back in pain as TC rolls him on his stomach and stands on the back of his legs. TC hooks the legs and reaches down and grabs KJ’s arms, pulling back in a surfboard type maneuver. KJ screams in pain as TC sneers at him. Dave: That is a painful submission move! Jeff: Why doesn’t this jobber save us all a lot of time and misery and just give up already? KJ does not give up and TC releases the hold. He pulls KJ to his feet and backs him into the ropes. TC whips KJ across and sets up for a back body drop. KJ comes off the ropes and kicks TC in the face. TC stands straight up and KJ follows up with a huge clothesline. KJ hops up on the second turnbuckle and awaits TC to get to his feet. TC is up and KJ jumps off and nails him with a Blockbuster. KJ makes a cover…1…2…NO! TC kicks out! Dave: A close call there. Jeff: Obviously not close enough. KJ drags TC to his feet and fires a couple of right hands to his face. KJ sends TC into the ropes, but TC reverses and sends KJ in. KJ holds onto the top rope to stop his momentum and TC charges at KJ who ducks and sends TC up and over onto one of the tables set up on the floor! Crowd: PCW!! PCW!! PCW!! Dave: OH MY GOD! TOMMY CHRISTOPHER JUST GOT BACK DROPPED OVER THE TOP ROPE TO THE OUTSIDE AND THROUGH A TABLE! KJ quickly climbs to the top turnbuckle overlooking the wreckage at ringside. He perches himself up top and puts his arms up in the air. KJ then flies off and LANDS AN ELBOW DROP FROM THE TOP TURNBUCKLE TO TC ON THE FLOOR!! Dave: HOLY SH*T!! Both men are down for several moments. Then amazingly TC starts to stir first. He stands and shakes his head a few times. Then he pulls KJ to his feet and rolls him into the ring. TC follows him in. Dave: This is incredible! Tommy Christopher just took all that punishment and he is the first one up! TC pulls KJ in and sets him up, then drops him with a piledriver. TC goes to the outside and climbs to the top turnbuckle. He steadies himself before he leaps off and lands a CORKSCREW MOONSAULT! TC makes a cover…1…2…NO! KJ is able to barely kick out! Karasu hits the mat, and SC flies to the top rope. Seconds later, SC comes flying off with a corkscrew moonsault onto Karasu. SC gets back to his feet and starts to go for the cover, bu the Seth Jackson hits him with a flying forearm. Jackson makes the cover on Karasu. 1... 2... 3!!!!! Dave: JACKSON HAS ELIMINATED KARASU WITH SHORT CIRCUIT'S SC-TV!!!!!!! Jeff: I think Jackson has just proved that this is his match. Dave: I think you just might be right. Short Circuit stands on the outside, trying to catch his breathe, but Seth Jackson follows him to the outside. Short Circuit starts to get to his feet, when Seth Jackson hits him with a standing dropkick. SC falls into the guardrail. Seth Jackson rushes at him, but Short Circuit gets a boot up to his face. Jacskon falls back. SC follows up with a clothesline, dropping Jackson to the arena floor. SC grabs Jackson up in a full nelson. He holds him there for a second, before dropping him with a full nelson slam to the floor. Dave: I think Da Westsidaz member just tried to break Seth Jackson in half. Jeff: I hate Da Westsidaz! Steve: I hate it when..... Dave: SHUT UP! SMACK! Short Circuit tosses Jackson into the ring. SC follows up with an elbow drop on Jackson. Short Circuit goes up to the top rope for the West4Life. He flies off, but Jackson steps out of the way. Dave: Short Circuit missed the missile dropkick on Jackson. Jeff: Die! Die! Die! Jackson sends SC into the ropes and drops him with a drop toe hold. Jackson goes up to the top rope and waits for SC to get to his feet. When SC does, Jackson came flying off witha top rope flipping neckbreaker. SC is nearly out. Dave: Jackson almost has this match put away. Jeff: Great, Riptah's out here now. Riptah hits the ring and clothesline's Jackson from behind. Riptah sends Jackson into the ropes. Jackson bounces off and almost gets hit with a kick from Riptah, but Jackson slides under it, and hits him with the a last ride/razor's edge type move. Dave: SETH JACKSON JUST HIT THE HITMAN BOMB ON RIPTAH!!!!!!!! SC starts to get to his feet, and land a kick on Jackson, but Jackson, the ever elusive cruiserweight, grabs his leg, and whips his leg, sending SC to the mat. Then Jackson grabs his legs and locks SC into the sharpshooter. Dave: JACKSON HAS LOCKED ON THE SHARPSHOOTER ON SHORT CIRCUIT!!!!!! SC tries and tries to get out, but soon thereafter, he taps out. Dave: Seth Jackson has retained his Cruiserweight title in dramatic fashion. Jeff Let’s get straight to the break. {PCW Action Figures. Public Health Warning. Do not use to stir any form of hot beverage. This includes all rednecks.} Dave: How very… informative. Jeff: Carry on? Dave: InDEED, let's go to the ring! Inside said ring Stephen Blood works over JJ....until he backs him into Tom Walczak. The massive Pollock slowly turns around....then grips JJ by the back of the neck, hurling him up and over the top rope! Steven: Noooooooooooooo...... Dan Hollywood tries to assault the Polish Punisher, but gets backhanded for his troubles. He provides enough of a distraction though for Blood to leap onto his shoulders with a sleeperhold! Jeff: Comrade Walzcak, dispose of this jobber! Seemingly hearingg him, Tom hurls Blood off and over his shoulder with great force. Jeff: Good job Comrade! Walczak begins stomping away at the Blood Brother....meanwhile JJ goes underneath the ring, grabbing his trusty chair. Crowd: He's hardcore! He's hardcore! In he goes.....lines up the shot.....then is spun around, kicked in the gut and DDT'd by Dan Hollywood! He goes for the cover.....one.....two......and two is all he gets! Steven: It'll take more than that to keep MY sex toy down. Jeff: (Shudders) Hollywood picks up JJ and sets him up on the top turnbuckle.....then grabs his chair. Dave: What's he trying to do!? DH wraps the chair around JJ's head....then gets behind him, throws a leg over him and takes him all the way to the ground with a fameasser, crunching his melon in the steel! Dave: OH MY GOD!!!!! Crowd: PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! JJ flops about like a fish with all it's flesh removed.....as DH mugs for the crowd though Stephen Blood sneaks up behind him, tries the STEPHENFLOW but gets shoved across the ring, right into the grasp of Tom Walzcak! Jeff: NOW CRUSH THE CAPITALIST PIG! Obeying, the Punisher hefts up Blood and puts him on the top turnbuckle....tries the POLISH PLANT but SB shifts his weight, turning it into a TOP ROPE STEPHENFLOW!!!! Crowd: PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! Walczak lies unmoving.....Blood stands over him and points. Blood: ASSCLOWN. Suddenly Mr Smiley Face leaps over the guard rail and charges the ring, before slamming a metal pipe over the back of Stephen Blood’s head, dropping him to the floor as Dan grabs JJ in a quick rollup… one… two… three! Winner: Dan Hollywood Jeff: What the hell is Smiley doing out here exactly? He’s got no business in this match! Mr Smiley grabs a microphone and rolls back in the ring, where now only a dazed JJ remains. Mr Smiley Face: Hello, Fargo! Cheap pop from the crowd. Mr Smiley Face: I bet you're all wondering that the hell I'm doing out here. I'm out here because I'm sick of what's going on here in the PCW. I'm sick of Tyler Nelson and his pack of overprivelidged goons running rampant through the front offices, changing the rules to suit them. I'm sick of Ivan Stanislav and his comrades coming out week after week and attacking everyone from behind like cowards. And I am TRULY sick of Clyde and his sick little crew. Clyde, you say that you are bringing a Dark Age to the PCW. Well, old friend, maybe I'll just have to bring a little light. With the help of some friends. Dave: Some friends? What is he talking about?! Jeff: Who cares? He's just some jobber with delusions of grandeur. Da MAN will take care of him! Mr Smiley Face unzips his leather jacket, and the crowd roars as it reveals an Asylum shirt. Dave: Mr Smiley Face is joining Asylum?! Mr Smiley Face: And now I give all of you a warning. Greed, Inc., the Red Army, Dark Ages. Don't play with fire... Mr Smiley Face pulls off his mask. Dave: Oh my god! It's the Trashcan Man!! The crowd roars it's approval as JJ stares at Trashcan Man in shock. TCM waits for the crowd to die down. Trashcan Man: You might get burned! "While The City Sleeps" by MC 900 Ft Jesus starts to play as Trashcan Man grins at JJ, pulling him into a rough hug before walking up the ramp with him. Steven: OH MY GOD! Jeff: What? Steven: All this time I thought it was Ellen Degenerates under that mask. Jeff: What the hell are you going on about Steven? Steven: Well it fits. Jeff: How? Steven: Have you ever seen a lesbian with PMS? Dave and Jeff go quiet. Jeff: Ok, Ellen Degenerates was in fact a good guess then. Dave: Let’s go to a break, right back after this it’s our main event of the evening. Jeff: Which is yet another, National Pride match. {Commercial: PCW Wet Dreams… this is a warning, this PPV does not feature Cal Seaver masturbating in any form or shape. Nor for that manner any masterabating in any form or shape} Steven: DAMN DAMN DAMN DAMN DAMN. You really can’t get the levels of masturbation on national television that you used to. Jeff: You used to? Steven: Oh yeah. Jeff: The Naturist Channel? Steven: How did you know that? Dave: Let’s clear this subject and let’s join Clay for our main event. Clay: The following contest is a National Pride submission style elimination match! Dave: You said a mouthfull. Steven: I gave him a mouthfull. Clay: (Glancing at the table) Ugh, take it away boys. Suddenly Miyagawa, Ivan Stanislav, Flaymin Aymin, Tony Stechino, Frisco, Sean Matthews and Vampir Nosferatu rush each other! Jeff: AHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHA!!!!!! Stanislav chases him out, as Frisco begins beating on Sean Matthews. He whips him off the ropes....tries a clothesline but gets ducked and dropped with a neckbreaker! Jeff: Isn't Matthews fired? Dave: You're asking me? I need liquor. Waiter! We move away from the ramblings of a very tired matchwriter working on a Dreamcast as Flaymin Aymin goes under the ring. SEAN MATTHEWS HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!!! Jeff: (Singing) God bless America..... Dave: You disgust me. The ref notices Aymin's unconcious state and counts him out! FLAYMIN AYMIN HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!!! On the outside of the ring Miyagawa has gained a temporary advantage over Stanislav.... leads him through the crowd as on the inside Vampir takes the fight to Frisco. He tosses the former champ to the ropes, follows him in with a knee to the gut and goes for the CUBAN UPRISING....only to have the DAMN member flip completely out of the powerbomb and dump him with the F-BOMB!!!! Steven: The Unknown Canadian! Jeff: The hell did you figure that one out Steven? Steven: .......I don't know. The man in a mask adorned with the Canadian Maple Leaf quickly steps over, locking Frisco in a Sharpshooter! The ref again notices a competitor's unconsious state and counts him out..... FRISCO HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!!! Dave: Hmph. Jeff: Gee Dave don't sound so enthused. On the outside Miyagawa has taken Stanislav all the way to the balcony....after much battling the goggle wearer dropkicks Ivan up and over the guardrail, all the way down to the floor far below....and Stanislav LANDS ON HIS FEET!!!!! Dave: OH MY GOD!!!!! High above Miya stares blankly.....then LAUNCHES himself off the balcony, all the way to Ivan below! Crowd: PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! Vampir clenches down on the throat of Stechino.....Tony frantically waves to the ref! TONY STECHINO HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!!! Jeff: Well what do we got, four left in this abmination? Dave: Yup. FINALLY Ivan and Miya get to their feet.....the Russian Bear LEVELING Miyagawa with a lariat before hauling his body back to the ring. Meanwhile in the ring Vampir turns his attention to the mysterious masked Canadian.....grins evily, causing the Canuck to back away in fear. Nosferatu throws a kick to his gut....only the Canadian grabs his foot, rolling him into the Canadian Maple Leaf!Dave: Hmm. Jeff: That booze putting you in a thoughtful mood Dave? Dave: Could be. The Bear sets up Miyagawa for AT DEATH'S DOOR.....when Dig by Mudvayne blares over the speakers. Jeff: Now he's got it! In the entryway appears Meanstreak himself, pounding his chest and roaring! The sound of the bell is heard, and Stanislav remembers the match and turns back to Naket, and slams him into the corner with a thunderous chokeslam… Naket falls face first to the mat seemingly unconscious. Stanslav grabs Naket in a headlock… the ref checks for signs of conciousness… there is none! THE WINNER OF THE MATCH.....IVAN STANISLAV!!!!! Dave: MY GOD, IVAN HAS WON THIS THING WITH A HEADLOCK! Stanslav dashes out of the ring and charges Streak, who catches him, turns and they both plumet off the stage, where Stanislav hits backfirst on a waiting speaker, while Meanstreak lands HEADFIRST on the concrete! Steven: Ouch ouch ouch! And both men GET UP as if it was nothing and continue to fight!!!! Dave: MY GOD IN HEAVEN, WE'LL BE BACK AT SABOTAGE!!!!! As the PCW2001 Logo appears in the bottom right of the screen, the blood drenched Miyagawa holds aloft his trophy… which appears to be what remains of Ricky Flame’s ear…
